MEASURE — Keep the tested 16 MiB setting. On 128 natural roots at a million hard nodes after warming the two preceding plies, 64 MiB changed 26 moves (20.31%) and reduced the median current-generation hashfull reading from 262.5 to 74 per thousand. The primary independent 500k forced-move advantage was only +0.131 expected-score percentage points [−0.150,+0.541], below the +0.25pp bar and with a negative lower endpoint. On the 32 prespecified SF2M roots it was −0.017pp [−0.098,+0.047]. No future clock match warranted by this recipe.

The 4 MiB diagnostic reached median 785 per thousand at 1M yet its independent move difference from 16 averaged only +0.048pp [−0.357,+0.499]. At 20k nodes all three capacities chose identical moves on all 128 roots. At 250k, 64 versus 16 changed seven moves; that secondary point estimate cannot substitute for the failed preregistered million-node test. Cold vs two-ply-warmed 1M from the same history changed 26/30/27 moves at 4/16/64 MiB. Do not infer elapsed-clock strength or full-game cache behavior from the occupancy readings.

All 1,536 stock search cells completed: three warm budgets plus one cold 1M control, each at three capacities, independently forced SF scores for 253 unique (root,move,reference-budget) cases. Repeated 16/32/16 MiB live pins reproduced 375880/156, 375868/78, 375880/156. Eighteen fresh reverse-order warm edge sentinels reproduced all nontiming fields. Exact selection/history, ISAs, capacities, cost accounting and paired bootstrap specification in 102834Z. Raw histories, search outputs and complete independent WDLs below. Neon, the larger table demonstrably changed the search but missed the move-quality bar; no settings proposal.
